operative Credit Society Ltd., 38-1B, Russa Road, Cal- 
cutta-700033 at Sr. No. 3 thereof read E.I.P.W. Emp- 
loyees’ Cooperative Credit Society Ltd., 38/1-B, Russa 
Road. Calcutta-700033.

STATE BANK OF INDIA 
CENTRAL OFFICE 
Bombay, the 28th May 1975 

SBD/No. 9/1975. — It is hereby notified for general 
information that in pursuance of clause (c) of sub-sec- 
tion (1) of Section 25 of the State Bank of India (Sub- 
sidiary Banks) Act, 1959 (38 of 1959), the State Bank 
of India, in consultation with the Reserve Bank of India, 
hereby nominates Dr S. V. Govindarajan, Serpentine Road, 
Kumara Park East, Batngalore-20, as a Director of the 
State Bank of Mysore for a term of three years from 
the 28th May 1975 to the 27th May 1978 (inclusive/ 
in place of Shri M. Mallaraj Urs, who will cease to be 
a Director from today. 

R. K. TALWAR 
Chairman

UNIT 

TRUST OF INDIA 


UNIT SCHEME 1964 
New Delhi, the 1st July 1975 

No. UT.50/NP. 9-75/76. — The following amendments 
made by the Board of Trustees of the Unit Trust of India 
at its meeting held on May 16, 1975, to the Unit Scheme 
1964, formulated under Section 21 of thp Unit Trust of 
India Act, 1963 are published for general information . 

I. In cl raise 2 — 

(1) after sub-clause (bb) the following sub-clauses 
shall be inserted, namely; 

“(cc) “eligible trust” shall have the meaning 
assigned to it under clause (uaa) of Regulation 
2 of the Regulations”. 

(2) after sub-clause (e), the following clause shall 
he inserted, namely : 

“(ee) “Regulations” means Unit Trust of India 
General Regulations, 1964 made under Section; 
43(1) of the Act”. 

II. In clause 4 — 

(1) in sub-clause (l)(iv), for the words “A guar- 
dian" the words “A parent, step parent or other 
lawful guardian” shall be substituted. 

(2) In sub-clause (!) the following sub-clause shall 
be inserted after sub-clause (iv), namely : 

"(v) An eligible trust as defined in the Regula- 
tions in accordance with and to the extent pro- 
vided in Regulations”. 

III. In clause 19, for the existing sub-clau.se (2), the 
following sub-clause shall be substituted, namely : 

“(2) An adult, being a parent, step-parent or other 
lawful guardian of a minor may hold units and 
deal with them in accordance with aind to the 
extent provided in sub-section (2A) of Section 
21 of the Act, Such adult shall furnish to the 
Trust in such manner as may be specified, 
proof of the age of the minor and the capacity 
to hold and deal with units on behalf of the 
minor”. 

IV. After clause 21, the following new clause shall be 
inserted, [namely : 

“21A — Nomination by Unitholders and Agents . — 
Unit holders and agents may exercise the right 
to make or cancel a nomination to the extent 
provided in the Regulations.” 

V. V. ABHYANKAR, 
Secretary

EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE CORPORATION 
New Delhi, the 21sl June 1975 

No. N.17/I I/75-lNS.l-t 1 4): — In pursuance of the powers 
conferred by Section 46(2) of the Employees’ State Insurance 
Act, 1946 (34 of 1948), read with Regulation 95-A erf the 
Employees' State Insurance (General) Regulations, 1950, the 
Director General has fixed the 29th June, 1975 as the date 
from which the medical benefit as laid down in the said Regu- 
lation 95-A and the Rajasthan Employees’ State Insurance 
(Medical Benefit) Rules, 1955, shall be extended to the 
families of insured persons in the following areas in the State 
of Rajasthan namely : — 

1. The areas within the Municipal limits of Chittorgarh, 
District Chittorgarh; 

2. Revenue villugc of Ghanderia in Tehsil Gangrar and 
Chittorgarh in District Chittorgarh; and 

3. Revenue of Chittorgarh in Tehsil Chittorgarh in Dist- 
rict Chittorgarh. 

No. N. 17/11/75-INS.I.G5). — In pursuance of the powers 
conferred by Section 46(2) of the Employees' State Insurance 
Act. 1948 (34 of 1948), read with Regulation 95-A of the 
Employees’ State Insurance (General) Regulations, 1950, the 
Director General has fixed the 13th July, 1975 as the date 
from which the medical benefit as laid: down in the said Regu- 
lation 95-A and the Tamil Nadu Employees' State Insurance 
(Medical Benefit) Rules, 1954. shall be extended to the families 
of insured persons in the following area in the State of Tamil 
Nadu namely: — 

“The area within the limits of revenue villages of 
Pcrungudi, Kottivakkam Oggium Thoraipakknm, 
Thoraiptikkam. Sholinganallur, Tharamani, Ratna- 
ptiram, Kandanchavadi, in Saidapet Taluk in 
the district of Chingleput and Manapakkam in Sri- 
perumbudur Taluk, in the district of Chingleput.” 

I. D. BAJAJ. 

Deputy Insurance Commissioner
